# Paper board

689 harness-level RSI papers scored. Score (0–100) = **E**vidence (50) + e**X**ecution against the pattern card (30) + evidence **L**evel (10) + **C**ode (10); every judgment is quote-backed — see [SCORE_SPEC](../research/SCORE_SPEC.md). It measures what a paper reports and how it executes its move, not the value of the idea.
